- **dba-mongo**: Use whenever the task involves MongoDB 7+ database design or administration: document schema design, ESR index rule, aggregation pipeline construction, sharding strategy, Atlas Search, change streams. Use for MongoDB — for relational databases use the appropriate dba-* worker. ([MassimilianoPili/agent-framework](https://github.com/MassimilianoPili/agent-framework/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/dba-mongo))
- **dba-mongodb**: MongoDB heuristics catalogue for the dba role. Detects indexing (ESR rule), modeling (embed vs reference), query, migration, retention (TTL), capacity (shard key), security, and concurrency problems. Every finding ships with a Mongo shell command. ([leocosta/octopus](https://github.com/leocosta/octopus/tree/HEAD/skills/dba-mongodb) · ★6)
- **dba-mssql**: MSSQL heuristics catalogue for the dba role. Detects indexing, modeling, query, migration, retention, capacity, security, and concurrency problems specific to SQL Server. Every finding ships with a T-SQL fix. ([leocosta/octopus](https://github.com/leocosta/octopus/tree/HEAD/skills/dba-mssql) · ★6)
- **dba-mssql**: Use whenever the task involves SQL Server 2022+ database design or administration: T-SQL stored procedures and functions, DMV performance monitoring, columnstore indexes, temporal tables, Always On availability groups. Use for SQL Server — for other databases use the matching dba-* worker. ([MassimilianoPili/agent-framework](https://github.com/MassimilianoPili/agent-framework/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/dba-mssql))
- **dba-mysql**: Use whenever the task involves MySQL 8.0/MariaDB 11 database design or administration: InnoDB schema design, Flyway migrations, EXPLAIN FORMAT=TREE analysis, full-text search, covering indexes, replication. Use for MySQL/MariaDB — for PostgreSQL use dba-postgres. ([MassimilianoPili/agent-framework](https://github.com/MassimilianoPili/agent-framework/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/dba-mysql))

- **dba-postgres**: PostgreSQL heuristics catalogue for the dba role. Detects indexing, modeling, query, migration, retention, capacity, security, and concurrency problems specific to Postgres. Every finding ships with a SQL fix. ([leocosta/octopus](https://github.com/leocosta/octopus/tree/HEAD/skills/dba-postgres) · ★6)
- **dba-postgres**: Use whenever the task involves PostgreSQL 16+ database design or administration: schema design, Flyway migrations, index strategy (B-tree, GIN, GiST, BRIN), query optimization via EXPLAIN ANALYZE, partitioning, extensions (pgvector, pg_trgm, PostGIS, AGE), roles and RLS. Use for PostgreSQL — for other databases use the matching dba-* worker. ([MassimilianoPili/agent-framework](https://github.com/MassimilianoPili/agent-framework/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/dba-postgres))
- **dba-postgres-senior**: Atua como DBA sênior especialista em PostgreSQL — modelagem de dados, migrações seguras sem downtime, índices, EXPLAIN e tuning de queries, VACUUM e bloat, pool de conexões, segurança e privilégios mínimos, multi-tenancy, backup/PITR, observabilidade e pgvector (busca vetorial e híbrida para RAG). Use esta skill sempre que a tarefa envolver banco de dados, schema, tabela, coluna, índice, constraint, migração (golang-migrate, Alembic), SQL, sqlc, pgx, query lenta, pgvector/embeddings, roles e permissões do Postgres, docker-compose do banco, backup, ou revisão de qualquer coisa que persista dad… ([bruno-blauzius/agent-planejador-pulverizacao](https://github.com/bruno-blauzius/agent-planejador-pulverizacao/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dba-postgres-senior))
